MA	<p>e81e_9290</p> <p>A phenotypically <i>wildtype</i> prospective couple seeks genetic counseling. The man has a <strong>balanced translocation</strong>, between chromosomes 4 and 15.This means that a segment of chromosome 4 has been exchanged with a segment of chromosome 15 without any gain or loss of genetic material. While balanced translocations typically do not affect the individual's phenotype, they can result in different types of gametes during reproduction.</p><p><ul><li><strong>Chromosome Pair:</strong> 4, 15</li><li><strong>Segregation Type:</strong> alternate</li></ul></p><table style="border-collapse: collapse;
